<div id="app"></div>
<script type="module">
// ===================== GUEST DOM SIMULATION =====================
class GuestNode {
  constructor() {
    this.parentNode = null;
    /** @type {GuestNode[]} */
    this.children = [];
  }
  appendChild(child) {
    if (child.parentNode) child.parentNode.removeChild(child);
    child.parentNode = this;
    this.children.push(child);
    return child;
  }
  removeChild(child) {
    const idx = this.children.indexOf(child);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      this.children.splice(idx, 1);
      child.parentNode = null;
    }
    return child;
  }
  insertBefore(newNode, refNode) {
    if (newNode.parentNode) newNode.parentNode.removeChild(newNode);
    const idx = refNode ? this.children.indexOf(refNode) : -1;
    if (idx === -1) {
      this.children.push(newNode);
    } else {
      this.children.splice(idx, 0, newNode);
    }
    newNode.parentNode = this;
    return newNode;
  }
}

class GuestElement extends GuestNode {
  constructor(tagName) {
    super();
    this.tagName = tagName;
    this.attributes = {};
    this._classList = new Set();
    this._style = {};
    this._listeners = {};
    this._textContent = '';
    this._value = '';
  }
  get id() { return this.attributes.id || ''; }
  set id(v) { if (v) this.attributes.id = v; else delete this.attributes.id; }
  get className() { return Array.from(this._classList).join(' '); }
  set className(v) { this._classList = new Set(String(v).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)); }
  get textContent() { return this._textContent; }
  set textContent(v) {
    this._textContent = String(v);
    this.children = [];
  }
  get value() { return this._value; }
  set value(v) { this._value = String(v); }
  get classList() {
    const self = this;
    return {
      add(...c) { c.forEach(x => x && self._classList.add(x)); },
      remove(...c) { c.forEach(x => self._classList.delete(x)); },
      toggle(c) { self._classList.has(c) ? self._classList.delete(c) : self._classList.add(c); },
      contains(c) { return self._classList.has(c); },
    };
  }
  get style() {
    const self = this;
    return new Proxy(self._style, {
      set(t, p, v) { t[p] = v; return true; }
    });
  }
  setAttribute(n, v) { this.attributes[n] = String(v); }
  getAttribute(n) { return this.attributes[n]; }
  removeAttribute(n) { delete this.attributes[n]; }
  addEventListener(event, handler) {
    if (!this._listeners[event]) this._listeners[event] = [];
    this._listeners[event].push(handler);
  }
  removeEventListener(event, handler) {
    if (!this._listeners[event]) return;
    this._listeners[event] = this._listeners[event].filter(h => h !== handler);
  }
}

class GuestDocument {
  constructor() {
    this.body = this.createElement('body');
  }
  createElement(tagName) { return new GuestElement(tagName); }
  createTextNode(text) {
    const el = new GuestElement('__text__');
    el.textContent = text;
    return el;
  }
  getElementById(id) { return this._findId(this.body, id); }
  _findId(node, id) {
    if (node.attributes && node.attributes.id === id) return node;
    for (const c of node.children) { const f = this._findId(c, id); if (f) return f; }
    return null;
  }
}

// ===================== HOST RENDERER =====================
class HostRenderer {
  constructor(realDoc) {
    this.realDoc = realDoc;
    this.map = new WeakMap();
  }
  render(guest) {
    if (guest.tagName === '__text__') {
      return this.realDoc.createTextNode(guest.textContent);
    }
    const real = this.realDoc.createElement(guest.tagName);
    this.map.set(guest, real);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(guest.attributes)) real.setAttribute(k, v);
    if (guest.tagName === 'input' || guest.tagName === 'textarea') real.value = guest.value;
    if (guest._classList.size) real.className = Array.from(guest._classList).join(' ');
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(guest._style)) real.style[k] = v;
    for (const [evt, handlers] of Object.entries(guest._listeners)) {
      handlers.forEach(h => real.addEventListener(evt, e => {
          const ge = {
          target: { value: e.target.value, checked: e.target.checked, dataset: e.target.dataset },
          preventDefault: () => e.preventDefault(),
          stopPropagation: () => e.stopPropagation(),
          key: e.key,
          metaKey: e.metaKey,
          ctrlKey: e.ctrlKey,
          shiftKey: e.shiftKey,
          dataTransfer: e.dataTransfer ? {
            getData: t => e.dataTransfer.getData(t),
            setData: (t, d) => e.dataTransfer.setData(t, d),
            effectAllowed: e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed,
          } : null,
        };
        h(ge);
      }));
    }
    for (const c of guest.children) real.appendChild(this.render(c));
    if (!guest.children.length && guest._textContent) real.textContent = guest._textContent;
    return real;
  }
  sync(guestRoot, realParent) {
    realParent.innerHTML = '';
    realParent.appendChild(this.render(guestRoot));
  }
}

// ===================== TODO APP =====================
let state = {
  todos: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('guest-todos') || '[]'),
  filter: 'all',
  editingId: null,
};

function save() {
  localStorage.setItem('guest-todos', JSON.stringify(state.todos));
}

function setState(patch) {
  state = { ...state, ...patch };
  save();
  render();
}

function nextId() {
  return Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5);
}

const doc = new GuestDocument();
const renderer = new HostRenderer(document);

function addTodo(text) {
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return;
  setState({ todos: [...state.todos, { id: nextId(), text: trimmed, completed: false, order: state.todos.length }] });
}

function buildApp() {
  const filtered = state.todos.filter(t =>
    state.filter === 'all' ? true :
    state.filter === 'active' ? !t.completed :
    t.completed
  );
  const activeCount = state.todos.filter(t => !t.completed).length;

  const app = doc.createElement('div');
  app.className = 'todoapp';

  const header = doc.createElement('header');
  header.className = 'header';

  const h1 = doc.createElement('h1');
  h1.textContent = 'todos';
  header.appendChild(h1);

  const inputWrap = doc.createElement('div');
  inputWrap.className = 'input-wrap';

  const input = doc.createElement('input');
  input.className = 'new-todo';
  input.setAttribute('placeholder', 'What needs to be done?');
  input.setAttribute('autofocus', 'true');
  input.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      addTodo(e.target.value);
      e.target.value = '';
    }
  });
  inputWrap.appendChild(input);

  const addBtn = doc.createElement('button');
  addBtn.className = 'add-btn';
  addBtn.textContent = 'Add';
  addBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const realInput = document.querySelector('.new-todo');
    if (realInput) {
      addTodo(realInput.value);
      realInput.value = '';
    }
  });
  inputWrap.appendChild(addBtn);

  header.appendChild(inputWrap);

  app.appendChild(header);

  if (state.todos.length) {
    const list = doc.createElement('ul');
    list.className = 'todo-list';
    list.id = 'todo-list';

    filtered.forEach((todo, index) => {
      const item = doc.createElement('li');
      item.className = 'todo-item' + (todo.completed ? ' completed' : '') + (state.editingId === todo.id ? ' editing' : '');
      item.setAttribute('draggable', 'true');
      item.setAttribute('data-id', todo.id);
      item.setAttribute('data-index', String(index));

      const handle = doc.createElement('span');
      handle.className = 'drag-handle';
      handle.textContent = '⋮⋮';
      handle.setAttribute('title', 'Drag to reorder');
      item.appendChild(handle);

      const toggle = doc.createElement('input');
      toggle.className = 'toggle';
      toggle.setAttribute('type', 'checkbox');
      if (todo.completed) toggle.setAttribute('checked', 'checked');
      toggle.addEventListener('change', () => {
        setState({
          todos: state.todos.map(t => t.id === todo.id ? { ...t, completed: !t.completed } : t)
        });
      });
      item.appendChild(toggle);

      const label = doc.createElement('label');
      label.textContent = todo.text;
      label.addEventListener('dblclick', () => setState({ editingId: todo.id }));
      item.appendChild(label);

      const edit = doc.createElement('textarea');
      edit.className = 'edit';
      edit.setAttribute('rows', '1');
      edit.value = todo.text;
      edit.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          const text = e.target.value.trim();
          if (text) {
            setState({
              todos: state.todos.map(t => t.id === todo.id ? { ...t, text } : t),
              editingId: null,
            });
          }
        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          setState({ editingId: null });
        }
      });
      edit.addEventListener('blur', e => {
        const text = e.target.value.trim();
        if (text) {
          setState({
            todos: state.todos.map(t => t.id === todo.id ? { ...t, text } : t),
            editingId: null,
          });
        }
      });
      item.appendChild(edit);

      const destroy = doc.createElement('button');
      destroy.className = 'destroy';
      destroy.textContent = '×';
      destroy.addEventListener('click', () => {
        setState({ todos: state.todos.filter(t => t.id !== todo.id) });
      });
      item.appendChild(destroy);

      // Drag events
      item.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
        e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', todo.id);
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragover', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
      });
      item.addEventListener('drop', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const draggedId = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
        if (draggedId === todo.id) return;
        const fromIdx = state.todos.findIndex(t => t.id === draggedId);
        const toIdx = state.todos.findIndex(t => t.id === todo.id);
        if (fromIdx === -1 || toIdx === -1) return;
        const next = [...state.todos];
        const [moved] = next.splice(fromIdx, 1);
        next.splice(toIdx, 0, moved);
        setState({ todos: next });
      });

      list.appendChild(item);
    });
    app.appendChild(list);

    const footer = doc.createElement('footer');
    footer.className = 'footer';

    const count = doc.createElement('span');
    count.className = 'todo-count';
    count.innerHTML = `<strong>${activeCount}</strong> item${activeCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} left`;
    footer.appendChild(count);

    const filters = doc.createElement('ul');
    filters.className = 'filters';
    const filtersData = [
      { label: 'All', val: 'all' },
      { label: 'Active', val: 'active' },
      { label: 'Completed', val: 'completed' },
    ];
    filtersData.forEach(f => {
      const li = doc.createElement('li');
      const a = doc.createElement('a');
      a.textContent = f.label;
      if (state.filter === f.val) a.className = 'selected';
      a.addEventListener('click', () => setState({ filter: f.val }));
      li.appendChild(a);
      filters.appendChild(li);
    });
    footer.appendChild(filters);

    const clearBtn = doc.createElement('button');
    clearBtn.className = 'clear-completed';
    clearBtn.textContent = 'Clear completed';
    if (!state.todos.some(t => t.completed)) clearBtn.className += ' hidden';
    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setState({ todos: state.todos.filter(t => !t.completed) });
    });
    footer.appendChild(clearBtn);

    app.appendChild(footer);
  }

  return app;
}

function render() {
  const guestRoot = buildApp();
  const realApp = document.getElementById('app');
  renderer.sync(guestRoot, realApp);
  if (state.editingId) {
    const input = realApp.querySelector('.todo-item.editing .edit');
    if (input) {
      input.focus();
      input.selectionStart = input.selectionEnd = input.value.length;
    }
  }
}

render();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
async function loadSites() {
  const res = await fetch('/api/sites');
  const sites = await res.json();
  const list = document.getElementById('site-list');
  if (sites.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = '<li class="empty">No sites configured</li>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = sites.map(s => `
    <li class="site-item">
      <div>
        <div class="site-name">${escapeHtml(s.subdomain)}.localhost</div>
        <div class="site-dir">${escapeHtml(s.directory)}</div>
      </div>
      <button class="remove-btn" data-subdomain="${escapeHtml(s.subdomain)}" title="Remove">×</button>
    </li>
  `).join('');

  list.querySelectorAll('.remove-btn').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const subdomain = btn.dataset.subdomain;
      await fetch('/api/sites', {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ subdomain }),
      });
      loadSites();
    });
  });
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
}

document.getElementById('add-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const subdomain = document.getElementById('subdomain').value.trim();
  const directory = document.getElementById('directory').value.trim();
  if (!subdomain || !directory) return;
  await fetch('/api/sites', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ subdomain, directory }),
  });
  document.getElementById('subdomain').value = '';
  document.getElementById('directory').value = '';
  loadSites();
});

loadSites();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<!-- dev/wasm-web-runtimes/examples/codemirror/fixtures/example.html -->
<!doctype html>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<title>Small article</title>
<article>
  <h1>A small article</h1>

<!-- dev/wasm-web-runtimes/examples/codemirror/full/index.html -->
<!doctype html>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Full CodeMirror UI in QuickJS</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="../demo.css">
<div id="editor"></div>
<div class="runtime-status" role="status"><span class="runtime-spinner" aria-label="Loading"></span></div>
<script type="module">
  import WasmWebMachine from "../wasm-web-machine.js";

  const status = document.querySelector(".runtime-status");
  try {
    const response = await fetch("../generated/codemirror-canonical.wasm");
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Wasm response ${response.status}`);
    const module = await WebAssembly.compileStreaming(response);
    const sections = WebAssembly.Module.customSections(module, "wasm-web-machine");
    const machine = new WasmWebMachine(module, document, {
      stamp: sections.length === 1 ? new Uint8Array(sections[0]) : undefined,
      services: {
        route: { get: () => "/", listen() {} },
        storage: { get: () => null, set() {}, delete() {}, listen() {} },
      },
    });
    await machine.onmsg(0);
    document.body.dataset.ready = "true";
    status.remove();
  } catch (error) {
    status.dataset.error = "";
    status.textContent = `QuickJS CodeMirror could not start: ${error?.message || error}`;
    throw error;
  }
</script>

<main id="workspace"></main>
<div class="runtime-status" role="status"><span class="runtime-spinner" aria-label="Loading"></span></div>
<script type="module">
  import WasmWebMachine from "../../wasm-web-machine.js";

  const status = document.querySelector(".runtime-status");
  try {

    const module = await WebAssembly.compileStreaming(response);
    const machine = new WasmWebMachine(module, document);
    await machine.onmsg(0);

<!-- dev/wasm-web-runtimes/examples/codemirror/mobile-syntax/microquickjs/index.html -->
<!doctype html>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Viewport-local syntax · MicroQuickJS</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="../../mobile-syntax.css">
<main id="workspace"></main>
<div class="runtime-status" role="status">Loading MicroQuickJS…</div>
<script type="module">
  import WasmWebMachine from "../../wasm-web-machine.js";
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  function namedCall(name, argument) {
    const label = encoder.encode(name);
    const value = encoder.encode(argument);
    const message = new Uint8Array(2 + label.length + value.length);
    message[0] = 2;
    message.set(label, 1);
    message.set(value, label.length + 2);
    return message;
  }
  const fixtureText = {};
  for (const mode of ["javascript", "css", "markdown", "html"])
    fixtureText[mode] = await (await fetch(
      `../../generated/mobile-fixture-${mode}.txt`)).text();
  const response = await fetch("../../generated/codemirror-mobile-syntax-microquickjs.wasm");
  const module = await WebAssembly.compileStreaming(response);
  const sections = WebAssembly.Module.customSections(module, "wasm-web-machine");
  const machine = new WasmWebMachine(module, document, {
    stamp: sections.length === 1 ? new Uint8Array(sections[0]) : undefined,
    services: {
      route: { get: () => "/", listen() {} },
      storage: { get: () => null, set() {}, delete() {}, listen() {} },
    },
  });
  await machine.onmsg(0);
  for (const [code, mode] of [["j", "javascript"], ["c", "css"],
      ["m", "markdown"], ["h", "html"]]) {
    await machine.onmsg(namedCall("loadMobileFixture", code + fixtureText[mode]));
    await machine.onmsg(new Uint8Array([255, 255, 255, 255]));
  }
  document.querySelector(".runtime-status").remove();
</script>

<!-- dev/wasm-web-runtimes/examples/microquickjs-suite/web/mahjong-example.html -->
<!doctype html>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<body>
<noscript>This application requires JavaScript and WebAssembly.</noscript>
<script type="module">
  import WasmWebMachine from "../wasm-web-machine.js";

  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const prefix = `-wwc--${location.pathname.replace(/\W+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-|-$/g, "") || "root"}--`;
  const area = (kind) => kind === "local" ? [localStorage, 8192] :
    kind === "session" ? [sessionStorage, 128 * 1024] : null;
  const key = (name) => prefix + name;
  const size = (storage, replacementKey, replacementValue) => {
    let bytes = 0;
    for (let index = 0; index < storage.length; index++) {
      const found = storage.key(index);
      if (!found?.startsWith(prefix) || found === replacementKey) continue;
      bytes += encoder.encode(found).length +
        encoder.encode(storage.getItem(found) || "").length;
    }
    return bytes + encoder.encode(replacementKey).length +
      encoder.encode(replacementValue).length;
  };
  const storage = {
    get(kind, name) { return area(kind)?.[0].getItem(key(name)) ?? null; },
    set(kind, name, value) {
      const selected = area(kind);
      if (!selected) throw new Error("Unknown storage area");
      const [storageArea, limit] = selected;
      const storageKey = key(name);
      if (size(storageArea, storageKey, value) > limit) {
        throw new Error(`${kind} storage quota exceeded`);
      }
      storageArea.setItem(storageKey, value);
    },
    delete(kind, name) { area(kind)?.[0].removeItem(key(name)); },
    listen(kind, name, callback) {
      const storageKey = key(name);
      addEventListener("storage", (event) => {
        if (kind === "local" && event.storageArea === localStorage &&
            event.key === storageKey) callback();
      });
    },
  };
  const allowFont = [
    "a0d054c4af557de20afd6ca59f47ab353bcaec49c63ff04b6c9d39d0f8910557",
    "22219fb90e3b9bd28debd1825fe8d9a0154a0e31f8b3fa601ac1a0a5aa5e6d1b",
    "d80df8ff5aecd299a61549f9e29ab1ed0b9b05f4ea71d50fe978e07d5240b235",
  ];
  const module = await WebAssembly.compileStreaming(fetch("./main.wasm"));
  const [stamp] = WebAssembly.Module.customSections(module, "wasm-web-machine");
  const machine = new WasmWebMachine(module, document, {
    allowFont,
    services: { storage },
    stamp: new Uint8Array(stamp),
  });
  await machine.onmsg(0);
</script>

<!-- examples/todo-matrix/source.html -->
<div id="app"></div>
<script type="module">
const STORAGE_KEY = "todo-matrix-state";
const SAMPLE = [
  ["Task", "Design", "Build", "Review", "Ship"],
  ["Header", "", "", "", ""],
  ["Schemas", "", "", "", ""],
  ["Playwright", "", "", "", ""],
  ["Commit", "", "", "", ""],
].map((row) => row.join("\t")).join("\n");

let model = load();

function el(tag, className, text) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) node.className = className;
  if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
  return node;
}

function splitLine(line, delimiter) {
  return line.split(delimiter).map((cell) => cell.trim());
}

function parseMatrix(text) {
  const lines = String(text || SAMPLE).split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const delimiter = lines.some((line) => line.includes("\t")) ? "\t" : ",";
  const rows = lines.map((line) => splitLine(line, delimiter));
  const width = Math.max(2, ...rows.map((row) => row.length));
  while (rows.length < 2) rows.push([]);
  rows.forEach((row) => { while (row.length < width) row.push(""); });
  const columns = rows[0].slice(1).map((value, index) => value || `Column ${index + 1}`);
  const rowHeaders = rows.slice(1).map((row, index) => row[0] || `Row ${index + 1}`);
  const cells = rowHeaders.map((_, rowIndex) => columns.map((__, columnIndex) => {
    const value = rows[rowIndex + 1][columnIndex + 1].toLowerCase();
    if (value === "x" || value === "1" || value === "yes" || value === "done" || value === "complete") return "done";
    if (value === "/" || value === "wip" || value === "doing" || value === "construction" || value === "under construction") return "doing";
    return "open";
  }));
  return { columns, rowHeaders, cells };
}

function load() {
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || "null");
    if (saved && saved.columns && saved.rowHeaders && saved.cells) return saved;
  } catch (_err) {}
  return null;
}

function save() {
  if (model) localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(model));
}

function normalizeState(value) {
  if (value === true || value === "done") return "done";
  if (value === "doing") return "doing";
  return "open";
}

function nextState(value) {
  const state = normalizeState(value);
  if (state === "open") return "doing";
  if (state === "doing") return "done";
  return "open";
}

function stateLabel(value) {
  const state = normalizeState(value);
  if (state === "done") return "complete";
  if (state === "doing") return "under construction";
  return "open";
}

function label(row, column, state) {
  return `${row} / ${column}: ${stateLabel(state)}`;
}

function renderEntry(root) {
  const shell = el("main", "matrix-app");
  const entry = el("section", "matrix-entry");
  entry.setAttribute("data-view", "entry");

  const copy = el("div", "entry-copy");
  copy.appendChild(el("p", "eyebrow", "Todo Matrix"));
  copy.appendChild(el("h1", "", "Paste a grid"));
  copy.appendChild(el("p", "", "Use tabs, or commas when the cells themselves do not contain commas. The first row and first column become headers."));

  const panel = el("div", "entry-panel");
  const textarea = el("textarea", "matrix-source");
  textarea.id = "matrix-source";
  textarea.setAttribute("placeholder", "Task\tDesign\tBuild\tShip\nHeader\t\t\t\nDocs\t\t\t");
  textarea.value = SAMPLE;
  const go = el("button", "go-button", "Go");
  go.id = "go-button";
  go.setAttribute("type", "button");
  go.addEventListener("click", () => {
    model = parseMatrix(go.value);
    save();
    render();
  });
  panel.appendChild(textarea);
  panel.appendChild(go);

  entry.appendChild(copy);
  entry.appendChild(panel);
  shell.appendChild(entry);
  root.appendChild(shell);
}

function renderBoard(root) {
  const shell = el("main", "matrix-app");
  const board = el("section", "matrix-board");
  board.setAttribute("data-view", "board");
  const head = el("div", "board-head");
  const restart = el("button", "restart-button");
  restart.id = "restart-button";
  restart.setAttribute("type", "button");
  restart.setAttribute("aria-label", "Start over");
  restart.setAttribute("title", "Start over");
  const restartIcon = el("span", "restart-icon");
  restartIcon.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  restartIcon.appendChild(el("span", "restart-page"));
  restartIcon.appendChild(el("span", "restart-plus"));
  const restartTip = el("span", "restart-tip", "Start over");
  restart.appendChild(restartIcon);
  restart.appendChild(restartTip);
  restart.addEventListener("click", () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    model = null;
    render();
  });
  head.appendChild(restart);

  const wrap = el("div", "matrix-wrap");
  const grid = el("div", "task-grid");
  grid.setAttribute("aria-label", "Todo matrix");
  const headRow = el("div", "matrix-row matrix-head-row");
  headRow.appendChild(el("div", "matrix-heading matrix-corner"));
  model.columns.forEach((column) => {
    const heading = el("div", "matrix-heading matrix-column-heading");
    heading.appendChild(el("span", "column-label", column));
    headRow.appendChild(heading);
  });
  grid.appendChild(headRow);

  model.rowHeaders.forEach((rowHeader, rowIndex) => {
    const row = el("div", "matrix-row");
    const rowHeading = el("div", "matrix-heading matrix-row-heading");
    rowHeading.appendChild(el("span", "row-label", rowHeader));
    row.appendChild(rowHeading);
    model.columns.forEach((column, columnIndex) => {
      const state = normalizeState(model.cells[rowIndex][columnIndex]);
      const button = el("button", "cell-toggle");
      button.setAttribute("type", "button");
      button.setAttribute("data-state", state);
      button.setAttribute("aria-pressed", state === "open" ? "false" : "true");
      button.setAttribute("aria-label", label(rowHeader, column, state));
      button.addEventListener("click", () => {
        model.cells[rowIndex][columnIndex] = nextState(model.cells[rowIndex][columnIndex]);
        save();
        render();
      });
      row.appendChild(button);
    });
    grid.appendChild(row);
  });
  wrap.appendChild(grid);
  board.appendChild(head);
  board.appendChild(wrap);
  shell.appendChild(board);
  root.appendChild(shell);
}

function render() {
  const root = document.getElementById("app");
  root.innerHTML = "";
  if (model) renderBoard(root);
  else renderEntry(root);
}

render();
</script>

<!-- examples/todo/index.html -->

<script type="module">
// ===================== GUEST DOM SIMULATION =====================
class GuestNode {
  constructor() {
    this.parentNode = null;
    /** @type {GuestNode[]} */
    this.children = [];
  }
  appendChild(child) {
    if (child.parentNode) child.parentNode.removeChild(child);
    child.parentNode = this;
    this.children.push(child);
    return child;
  }
  removeChild(child) {
    const idx = this.children.indexOf(child);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      this.children.splice(idx, 1);
      child.parentNode = null;
    }
    return child;
  }
  insertBefore(newNode, refNode) {
    if (newNode.parentNode) newNode.parentNode.removeChild(newNode);
    const idx = refNode ? this.children.indexOf(refNode) : -1;
    if (idx === -1) {
      this.children.push(newNode);
    } else {
      this.children.splice(idx, 0, newNode);
    }
    newNode.parentNode = this;
    return newNode;
  }
}

class GuestElement extends GuestNode {
  constructor(tagName) {
    super();
    this.tagName = tagName;
    this.attributes = {};
    this._classList = new Set();
    this._style = {};
    this._listeners = {};
    this._textContent = '';
    this._value = '';
  }
  get id() { return this.attributes.id || ''; }
  set id(v) { if (v) this.attributes.id = v; else delete this.attributes.id; }
  get className() { return Array.from(this._classList).join(' '); }
  set className(v) { this._classList = new Set(String(v).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)); }
  get textContent() { return this._textContent; }
  set textContent(v) {
    this._textContent = String(v);
    this.children = [];
  }
  get value() { return this._value; }
  set value(v) { this._value = String(v); }
  get classList() {
    const self = this;
    return {
      add(...c) { c.forEach(x => x && self._classList.add(x)); },
      remove(...c) { c.forEach(x => self._classList.delete(x)); },
      toggle(c) { self._classList.has(c) ? self._classList.delete(c) : self._classList.add(c); },
      contains(c) { return self._classList.has(c); },
    };
  }
  get style() {
    const self = this;
    return new Proxy(self._style, {
      set(t, p, v) { t[p] = v; return true; }
    });
  }
  setAttribute(n, v) { this.attributes[n] = String(v); }
  getAttribute(n) { return this.attributes[n]; }
  removeAttribute(n) { delete this.attributes[n]; }
  addEventListener(event, handler) {
    if (!this._listeners[event]) this._listeners[event] = [];
    this._listeners[event].push(handler);
  }
  removeEventListener(event, handler) {
    if (!this._listeners[event]) return;
    this._listeners[event] = this._listeners[event].filter(h => h !== handler);
  }
}

class GuestDocument {
  constructor() {
    this.body = this.createElement('body');
  }
  createElement(tagName) { return new GuestElement(tagName); }
  createTextNode(text) {
    const el = new GuestElement('__text__');
    el.textContent = text;
    return el;
  }
  getElementById(id) { return this._findId(this.body, id); }
  _findId(node, id) {
    if (node.attributes && node.attributes.id === id) return node;
    for (const c of node.children) { const f = this._findId(c, id); if (f) return f; }
    return null;
  }
}

// ===================== HOST RENDERER =====================
class HostRenderer {
  constructor(realDoc) {
    this.realDoc = realDoc;
    this.map = new WeakMap();
  }
  render(guest) {
    if (guest.tagName === '__text__') {
      return this.realDoc.createTextNode(guest.textContent);
    }
    const real = this.realDoc.createElement(guest.tagName);
    this.map.set(guest, real);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(guest.attributes)) real.setAttribute(k, v);
    if (guest.tagName === 'input' || guest.tagName === 'textarea') real.value = guest.value;
    if (guest._classList.size) real.className = Array.from(guest._classList).join(' ');
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(guest._style)) real.style[k] = v;
    for (const [evt, handlers] of Object.entries(guest._listeners)) {
      handlers.forEach(h => real.addEventListener(evt, e => {
          const ge = {
          target: { value: e.target.value, checked: e.target.checked, dataset: e.target.dataset },
          preventDefault: () => e.preventDefault(),
          stopPropagation: () => e.stopPropagation(),
          key: e.key,
          metaKey: e.metaKey,
          ctrlKey: e.ctrlKey,
          shiftKey: e.shiftKey,
          dataTransfer: e.dataTransfer ? {
            getData: t => e.dataTransfer.getData(t),
            setData: (t, d) => e.dataTransfer.setData(t, d),
            effectAllowed: e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed,
          } : null,
        };
        h(ge);
      }));
    }
    for (const c of guest.children) real.appendChild(this.render(c));
    if (!guest.children.length && guest._textContent) real.textContent = guest._textContent;
    return real;
  }
  sync(guestRoot, realParent) {
    realParent.innerHTML = '';
    realParent.appendChild(this.render(guestRoot));
  }
}

// ===================== TODO APP =====================
let state = {
  todos: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('guest-todos') || '[]'),
  filter: 'all',
  editingId: null,
};

function save() {
  localStorage.setItem('guest-todos', JSON.stringify(state.todos));
}

function setState(patch) {
  state = { ...state, ...patch };
  save();
  render();
}

function nextId() {
  return Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5);
}

const doc = new GuestDocument();
const renderer = new HostRenderer(document);

function addTodo(text) {
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return;
  setState({ todos: [...state.todos, { id: nextId(), text: trimmed, completed: false, order: state.todos.length }] });
}

function buildApp() {
  const filtered = state.todos.filter(t =>
    state.filter === 'all' ? true :
    state.filter === 'active' ? !t.completed :
    t.completed
  );
  const activeCount = state.todos.filter(t => !t.completed).length;

  const app = doc.createElement('div');
  app.className = 'todoapp';

  const header = doc.createElement('header');
  header.className = 'header';

  const h1 = doc.createElement('h1');
  h1.textContent = 'todos';
  header.appendChild(h1);

  const inputWrap = doc.createElement('div');
  inputWrap.className = 'input-wrap';

  const input = doc.createElement('input');
  input.className = 'new-todo';
  input.setAttribute('placeholder', 'What needs to be done?');
  input.setAttribute('autofocus', 'true');
  input.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      addTodo(e.target.value);
      e.target.value = '';
    }
  });
  inputWrap.appendChild(input);

  const addBtn = doc.createElement('button');
  addBtn.className = 'add-btn';
  addBtn.textContent = 'Add';
  addBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const realInput = document.querySelector('.new-todo');
    if (realInput) {
      addTodo(realInput.value);
      realInput.value = '';
    }
  });
  inputWrap.appendChild(addBtn);

  header.appendChild(inputWrap);

  app.appendChild(header);

  if (state.todos.length) {
    const list = doc.createElement('ul');
    list.className = 'todo-list';
    list.id = 'todo-list';

    filtered.forEach((todo, index) => {
      const item = doc.createElement('li');
      item.className = 'todo-item' + (todo.completed ? ' completed' : '') + (state.editingId === todo.id ? ' editing' : '');
      item.setAttribute('draggable', 'true');
      item.setAttribute('data-id', todo.id);
      item.setAttribute('data-index', String(index));

      const handle = doc.createElement('span');
      handle.className = 'drag-handle';
      handle.textContent = '⋮⋮';
      handle.setAttribute('title', 'Drag to reorder');
      item.appendChild(handle);

      const toggle = doc.createElement('input');
      toggle.className = 'toggle';
      toggle.setAttribute('type', 'checkbox');
      if (todo.completed) toggle.setAttribute('checked', 'checked');
      toggle.addEventListener('change', () => {
        setState({
          todos: state.todos.map(t => t.id === todo.id ? { ...t, completed: !t.completed } : t)
        });
      });
      item.appendChild(toggle);

      const label = doc.createElement('label');
      label.textContent = todo.text;
      label.addEventListener('dblclick', () => setState({ editingId: todo.id }));
      item.appendChild(label);

      const edit = doc.createElement('textarea');
      edit.className = 'edit';
      edit.setAttribute('rows', '1');
      edit.value = todo.text;
      edit.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          const text = e.target.value.trim();
          if (text) {
            setState({
              todos: state.todos.map(t => t.id === todo.id ? { ...t, text } : t),
              editingId: null,
            });
          }
        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          setState({ editingId: null });
        }
      });
      edit.addEventListener('blur', e => {
        const text = e.target.value.trim();
        if (text) {
          setState({
            todos: state.todos.map(t => t.id === todo.id ? { ...t, text } : t),
            editingId: null,
          });
        }
      });
      item.appendChild(edit);

      const destroy = doc.createElement('button');
      destroy.className = 'destroy';
      destroy.textContent = '×';
      destroy.addEventListener('click', () => {
        setState({ todos: state.todos.filter(t => t.id !== todo.id) });
      });
      item.appendChild(destroy);

      // Drag events
      item.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
        e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', todo.id);
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
      });
      item.addEventListener('dragover', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
      });
      item.addEventListener('drop', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const draggedId = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
        if (draggedId === todo.id) return;
        const fromIdx = state.todos.findIndex(t => t.id === draggedId);
        const toIdx = state.todos.findIndex(t => t.id === todo.id);
        if (fromIdx === -1 || toIdx === -1) return;
        const next = [...state.todos];
        const [moved] = next.splice(fromIdx, 1);
        next.splice(toIdx, 0, moved);
        setState({ todos: next });
      });

      list.appendChild(item);
    });
    app.appendChild(list);

    const footer = doc.createElement('footer');
    footer.className = 'footer';

    const count = doc.createElement('span');
    count.className = 'todo-count';
    count.innerHTML = `<strong>${activeCount}</strong> item${activeCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} left`;
    footer.appendChild(count);

    const filters = doc.createElement('ul');
    filters.className = 'filters';
    const filtersData = [
      { label: 'All', val: 'all' },
      { label: 'Active', val: 'active' },
      { label: 'Completed', val: 'completed' },
    ];
    filtersData.forEach(f => {
      const li = doc.createElement('li');
      const a = doc.createElement('a');
      a.textContent = f.label;
      if (state.filter === f.val) a.className = 'selected';
      a.addEventListener('click', () => setState({ filter: f.val }));
      li.appendChild(a);
      filters.appendChild(li);
    });
    footer.appendChild(filters);

    const clearBtn = doc.createElement('button');
    clearBtn.className = 'clear-completed';
    clearBtn.textContent = 'Clear completed';
    if (!state.todos.some(t => t.completed)) clearBtn.className += ' hidden';
    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setState({ todos: state.todos.filter(t => !t.completed) });
    });
    footer.appendChild(clearBtn);

    app.appendChild(footer);
  }

  return app;
}

function render() {
  const guestRoot = buildApp();
  const realApp = document.getElementById('app');
  renderer.sync(guestRoot, realApp);
  if (state.editingId) {
    const input = realApp.querySelector('.todo-item.editing .edit');
    if (input) {
      input.focus();
      input.selectionStart = input.selectionEnd = input.value.length;
    }
  }
}

render();
</script>
</body>
</html>
